## 3. Open decisions

## 4. Recall by stratum

The estimate of each stratum is a log-linear model of the capture histories, with the catalogues that share lineage merged into one capture. The interval is the 95% interval of a bootstrap. A stratum uses at most 6 captures. A stratum with fewer than three captures takes the estimate of an independent pair of the census (`reports/resources-census.md`), and the model column names the pair.

| Kind | Decade | Resources | Captures | Model | Estimate | Interval | Recall | Recall at least | Target |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|

## 9. What cannot be claimed

The catalogue can show exactly that each record of each source has a resource with an outcome, that each reference is resolved or listed, and that each work that a curriculum names has a resource. It can give the recall of each stratum only as an estimate with an interval. It cannot show that no work exists that no catalogue, no bibliography and no course lists (RS11.1).

In the 4 strata with an estimate, the estimate of the works that no source lists is 6562958, with the interval 5933176 to 7410150. The strata with no estimate are not in this number.
